+void FGBPixelVfxClip::ComputeQuadAndLightPlacement(const FGBPixelVfxClip& Clip, float SizeScale,
+	const FVector& ComponentLocation, const FQuat& R,
+	FVector& OutQuadLocation, FVector& OutLightLocation)
+{
+	const float W = Clip.WorldWidthCm * SizeScale;
+	const float H = Clip.WorldHeightCm * SizeScale;
+	// Quad-local offset (cm) of the pivot point from the quad center.
+	const FVector PivotLocal((Clip.PivotU - 0.5f) * W, (0.5f - Clip.PivotV) * H, 0.f);
+	// Quad-local offset (cm) from the pivot point to the light anchor.
+	const FVector AnchorOffset((Clip.Light.AnchorU - Clip.PivotU) * W,
+		(Clip.PivotV - Clip.Light.AnchorV) * H, 0.f);
+	OutQuadLocation = ComponentLocation - R.RotateVector(PivotLocal);
+	// 10 cm toward the camera (along the quad normal) so the light is not inside the quad plane.
+	OutLightLocation = ComponentLocation + R.RotateVector(AnchorOffset)
+		+ R.RotateVector(FVector(0.f, 0.f, 10.f));
+}

+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxQuadPivotTest, "GBPixelVfx.Clip.QuadPivotPlacement",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBPixelVfxQuadPivotTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	FGBPixelVfxClip Clip;
+	Clip.WorldWidthCm = 200.f;
+	Clip.WorldHeightCm = 400.f;
+	Clip.PivotU = 0.5f;
+	Clip.PivotV = 1.f;
+	Clip.Light.AnchorU = 0.5f;
+	Clip.Light.AnchorV = 0.5f;
+	// Upright quad: local X = world X, local Y = world Z (up), normal faces -X.
+	const FQuat R(FMatrix(FVector(0.f, -1.f, 0.f), FVector(0.f, 0.f, 1.f), FVector(-1.f, 0.f, 0.f),
+		FVector::ZeroVector).Rotator());
+	const FVector Comp(100.f, 200.f, 0.f);
+	FVector QuadLoc;
+	FVector LightLoc;
+	FGBPixelVfxClip::ComputeQuadAndLightPlacement(Clip, 1.f, Comp, R, QuadLoc, LightLoc);
+	// PivotV = 1 (bottom of the frame): the quad center is H/2 above the component.
+	TestTrue(TEXT("pivot bottom puts the quad center H/2 up"),
+		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(QuadLoc.Z, 200.f, 0.01f));
+	TestTrue(TEXT("pivot bottom does not shift sideways"),
+		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(QuadLoc.X, Comp.X, 0.01f)
+		&& FMath::IsNearlyEqual(QuadLoc.Y, Comp.Y, 0.01f));
+	return true;
+}

+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxLightAnchorTest, "GBPixelVfx.Clip.LightAnchorPlacement",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBPixelVfxLightAnchorTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	FGBPixelVfxClip Clip;
+	Clip.WorldWidthCm = 200.f;
+	Clip.WorldHeightCm = 400.f;
+	Clip.PivotU = 0.5f;
+	Clip.PivotV = 1.f;
+	Clip.Light.AnchorU = 0.5f;
+	Clip.Light.AnchorV = 0.5f;
+	// Same upright frame as the pivot test.
+	const FQuat R(FMatrix(FVector(0.f, -1.f, 0.f), FVector(0.f, 0.f, 1.f), FVector(-1.f, 0.f, 0.f),
+		FVector::ZeroVector).Rotator());
+	const FVector Comp(100.f, 200.f, 0.f);
+	FVector QuadLoc;
+	FVector LightLoc;
+	FGBPixelVfxClip::ComputeQuadAndLightPlacement(Clip, 1.f, Comp, R, QuadLoc, LightLoc);
+	// AnchorV = 0.5 is H/2 above the pivot: the light sits H/2 above the component,
+	// pushed 10 cm toward the camera (quad normal = -X here).
+	TestTrue(TEXT("mid-frame anchor puts the light H/2 above the pivot"),
+		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(LightLoc.Z, 200.f, 0.01f));
+	TestTrue(TEXT("the light keeps the component Y"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(LightLoc.Y, Comp.Y, 0.01f));
+	TestTrue(TEXT("the light is pushed 10 cm toward the camera"),
+		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(LightLoc.X, Comp.X - 10.f, 0.01f));
+	return true;
+}

 // A transparent pixel whose 4 neighbors are all solid becomes solid with the most common neighbor color.
 GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void FillOnePixelHoles(FFrameMap& Map);
 
+// Every 4-connected region of solid pixels with the same ramp and index that is
+// smaller than MinPixels is recolored with the most common color among the solid
+// pixels 4-adjacent to the region (ties: the darker one). No-op when MinPixels <= 1.
+// Regions are found on the state before the pass (one pass).
+GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void RecolorSmallColorClusters(FFrameMap& Map, const FSettings& Settings, int32 MinPixels);
+
+// Fade-out for one-shot clips (no-op when bLoop or FadeOutFrames <= 0). Over the last
+// FadeOutFrames frames progress t goes from 1/(N+1) to N/(N+1). Dissolve removes a solid
+// pixel when Bayer4x4(x,y) < t; Erode removes solid pixels within ceil(t*MaxDepth)
+// 4-connected steps of a transparent pixel or the frame edge.
+GBPIXELVFXBAKER_API void ApplyFadeOut(TArray<FFrameMap>& Maps, const FSettings& Settings);
+
 // Transparent pixels 4-adjacent to a solid pixel become the outline color. Returns false for "None".

+void RecolorSmallColorClusters(FFrameMap& Map, const FSettings& Settings, int32 MinPixels)
+{
+	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
+	if (MinPixels <= 1 || Map.Width <= 0 || Map.Height <= 0
+		|| Map.Pixels.Num() != Map.Width * Map.Height)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	const int32 W = Map.Width;
+	const int32 H = Map.Height;
+	const TArray<FMappedPixel> Orig = Map.Pixels;
+	TArray<uint8> Seen;
+	Seen.SetNumZeroed(W * H);
+	const int32 DX[4] = { 1, -1, 0, 0 };
+	const int32 DY[4] = { 0, 0, 1, -1 };
+	TArray<int32> Island;
+	TArray<int32> Stack;
+	for (int32 Start = 0; Start < W * H; ++Start)
+	{
+		if (!Orig[Start].bSolid || Seen[Start])
+		{
+			continue;
+		}
+		Island.Reset();
+		Stack.Reset();
+		Stack.Add(Start);
+		Seen[Start] = 1;
+		while (Stack.Num() > 0)
+		{
+			const int32 At = Stack.Pop();
+			Island.Add(At);
+			const int32 X = At % W;
+			const int32 Y = At / W;
+			for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
+			{
+				const int32 NX = X + DX[d];
+				const int32 NY = Y + DY[d];
+				if (!Inside(NX, NY, W, H))
+				{
+					continue;
+				}
+				const int32 NAt = NY * W + NX;
+				if (!Seen[NAt] && Orig[NAt].bSolid
+					&& Orig[NAt].Ramp == Orig[Start].Ramp && Orig[NAt].Index == Orig[Start].Index)
+				{
+					Seen[NAt] = 1;
+					Stack.Add(NAt);
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		if (Island.Num() >= MinPixels)
+		{
+			continue;
+		}
+		// Border: solid 4-neighbors of the region with a different color (found on the pre-pass state).
+		TArray<int32> Border;
+		for (int32 At : Island)
+		{
+			const int32 X = At % W;
+			const int32 Y = At / W;
+			for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
+			{
+				const int32 NX = X + DX[d];
+				const int32 NY = Y + DY[d];
+				if (!Inside(NX, NY, W, H) || !Orig[NY * W + NX].bSolid)
+				{
+					continue;
+				}
+				const int32 NAt = NY * W + NX;
+				if (Orig[NAt].Ramp == Orig[Start].Ramp && Orig[NAt].Index == Orig[Start].Index)
+				{
+					continue;
+				}
+				Border.Add(NAt);
+			}
+		}
+		if (Border.Num() == 0)
+		{
+			continue;
+		}
+		uint8 BestRamp = Orig[Border[0]].Ramp;
+		uint8 BestIndex = Orig[Border[0]].Index;
+		int32 BestVotes = -1;
+		float BestLum = FLT_MAX;
+		for (int32 a = 0; a < Border.Num(); ++a)
+		{
+			const uint8 AR = Orig[Border[a]].Ramp;
+			const uint8 AI = Orig[Border[a]].Index;
+			bool bCounted = false;
+			for (int32 b = 0; b < a; ++b)
+			{
+				if (Orig[Border[b]].Ramp == AR && Orig[Border[b]].Index == AI)
+				{
+					bCounted = true;
+					break;
+				}
+			}
+			if (bCounted)
+			{
+				continue;
+			}
+			int32 VotesForA = 0;
+			for (int32 b = 0; b < Border.Num(); ++b)
+			{
+				if (Orig[Border[b]].Ramp == AR && Orig[Border[b]].Index == AI)
+				{
+					++VotesForA;
+				}
+			}
+			float Lum = FLT_MAX;
+			if (AR < Settings.Ramps.Num() && AI < Settings.Ramps[AR].Num())
+			{
+				Lum = PixelLuminance(FLinearColor(Settings.Ramps[AR][AI]));
+			}
+			if (VotesForA > BestVotes || (VotesForA == BestVotes && Lum < BestLum))
+			{
+				BestVotes = VotesForA;
+				BestLum = Lum;
+				BestRamp = AR;
+				BestIndex = AI;
+			}
+		}
+		for (int32 At : Island)
+		{
+			Map.Pixels[At].Ramp = BestRamp;
+			Map.Pixels[At].Index = BestIndex;
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+void ApplyFadeOut(TArray<FFrameMap>& Maps, const FSettings& Settings)
+{
+	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvertLocal;
+	if (Settings.bLoop || Settings.FadeOutFrames <= 0 || Maps.Num() == 0)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	const int32 N = FMath::Min(Settings.FadeOutFrames, Maps.Num());
+	const bool bErode = Settings.FadeOutMode.Equals(TEXT("Erode"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
+	static const uint8 Bayer[4][4] = { { 0, 8, 2, 10 }, { 12, 4, 14, 6 }, { 3, 11, 1, 9 }, { 15, 7, 13, 5 } };
+	for (int32 k = 0; k < N; ++k)
+	{
+		const int32 f = Maps.Num() - N + k;
+		FFrameMap& Map = Maps[f];
+		if (Map.Width <= 0 || Map.Height <= 0 || Map.Pixels.Num() != Map.Width * Map.Height)
+		{
+			continue;
+		}
+		const float T = (static_cast<float>(k) + 1.f) / (static_cast<float>(N) + 1.f);
+		if (!bErode)
+		{
+			for (int32 y = 0; y < Map.Height; ++y)
+			{
+				for (int32 x = 0; x < Map.Width; ++x)
+				{
+					const int32 At = y * Map.Width + x;
+					if (!Map.Pixels[At].bSolid)
+					{
+						continue;
+					}
+					const float Gate = static_cast<float>(Bayer[y % 4][x % 4]) / 16.f;
+					if (Gate < T)
+					{
+						Map.Pixels[At].bSolid = false;
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		else
+		{
+			const int32 W = Map.Width;
+			const int32 H = Map.Height;
+			TArray<int32> Dist;
+			Dist.SetNum(W * H);
+			for (int32 i = 0; i < W * H; ++i)
+			{
+				Dist[i] = -1;
+			}
+			TArray<int32> Queue;
+			Queue.Reserve(W * H);
+			int32 QHead = 0;
+			for (int32 i = 0; i < W * H; ++i)
+			{
+				if (!Map.Pixels[i].bSolid)
+				{
+					Dist[i] = 0;
+					Queue.Add(i);
+				}
+			}
+			// The frame edge counts as transparent: edge pixels are one step away.
+			for (int32 y = 0; y < H; ++y)
+			{
+				for (int32 x = 0; x < W; ++x)
+				{
+					if (x != 0 && y != 0 && x != W - 1 && y != H - 1)
+					{
+						continue;
+					}
+					const int32 At = y * W + x;
+					if (Map.Pixels[At].bSolid && Dist[At] < 0)
+					{
+						Dist[At] = 1;
+						Queue.Add(At);
+					}
+				}
+			}
+			const int32 DXE[4] = { 1, -1, 0, 0 };
+			const int32 DYE[4] = { 0, 0, 1, -1 };
+			while (QHead < Queue.Num())
+			{
+				const int32 At = Queue[QHead++];
+				const int32 X = At % W;
+				const int32 Y = At / W;
+				for (int32 d = 0; d < 4; ++d)
+				{
+					const int32 NX = X + DXE[d];
+					const int32 NY = Y + DYE[d];
+					if (!Inside(NX, NY, W, H))
+					{
+						continue;
+					}
+					const int32 NAt = NY * W + NX;
+					if (Dist[NAt] < 0 && Map.Pixels[NAt].bSolid)
+					{
+						Dist[NAt] = Dist[At] + 1;
+						Queue.Add(NAt);
+					}
+				}
+			}
+			int32 MaxDepth = 0;
+			for (int32 i = 0; i < W * H; ++i)
+			{
+				if (Map.Pixels[i].bSolid)
+				{
+					MaxDepth = FMath::Max(MaxDepth, Dist[i] < 0 ? 1 : Dist[i]);
+				}
+			}
+			if (MaxDepth <= 0)
+			{
+				continue;
+			}
+			const int32 KillDist = FMath::CeilToInt(T * static_cast<float>(MaxDepth));
+			for (int32 i = 0; i < W * H; ++i)
+			{
+				if (Map.Pixels[i].bSolid && Dist[i] >= 0 && Dist[i] <= KillDist)
+				{
+					Map.Pixels[i].bSolid = false;
+				}
+			}
+		}
+	}
+}
+
 bool ResolveOutlineColor(const FSettings& Settings, FColor& OutColor)
 {
 	if (Settings.Outline.Equals(TEXT("Black"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
 	{
System.Management.Automation.RemoteException

+	// The FluidFire fire ramp: used when a job has no Ramps array at all.
+	TArray<FColor> DefaultFireRamp()
+	{
+		TArray<FColor> Ramp;
+		const TCHAR* Hex[] = { TEXT("#4B0F0F"), TEXT("#BC2A12"), TEXT("#F03416"),
+			TEXT("#F3610B"), TEXT("#E2DE9C"), TEXT("#F5F3CF") };
+		for (const TCHAR* H : Hex)
+		{
+			FColor Srgb;
+			if (GBPixelVfxConvert::ParseHexColor(FString(H), Srgb))
+			{
+				Ramp.Add(Srgb);
+			}
+		}
+		return Ramp;
+	}

+		const float FramingHeightKeys = FramingWidthCm * Job.FrameHeight / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(1, Job.FrameWidth));
+		const FVector RelO = FVector::ZeroVector - FramingCenterCm;

+		Frame.Height = 1;

+		const FFrameMap Map = MapFrame(Frame, Settings, 1.f, 1.f, 1.f);
+		FColor Resolved(0, 0, 0, 0);
+		TArray<TArray<FColor>> Pixels;
+		ResolveFrames({ Map }, Settings, Pixels);
+		return RampIndexOf(Settings, Pixels[0][0]);
+	};

 	return true;

+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxColorClusterTest, "GBPixelVfx.Cleanup.ColorClusters",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBPixelVfxColorClusterTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
+	FSettings Settings = GBPixelVfxTestLocal::MakeRampSettings();
+	// Two colors in ramp 0; the frame is all color 1 with a 2-pixel color-2 speck.
+	FFrameMap Map;
+	Map.Width = 6;
+	Map.Height = 4;
+	Map.Pixels.SetNum(24);
+	for (int32 i = 0; i < 24; ++i)
+	{
+		Map.Pixels[i].bSolid = true;
+		Map.Pixels[i].Ramp = 0;
+		Map.Pixels[i].Index = 1;
+		Map.Pixels[i].Value = 0.5f;
+	}
+	Map.Pixels[7].Index = 2;
+	Map.Pixels[8].Index = 2;
+	FFrameMap Cleaned = Map;
+	RecolorSmallColorClusters(Cleaned, Settings, 3);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("a 2-pixel speck is recolored when the minimum is 3"), Cleaned.SolidCount(), 24);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("the speck takes the surrounding color"), Cleaned.Pixels[7].Index, 1);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("the speck keeps its ramp"), Cleaned.Pixels[7].Ramp, 0);
+	FFrameMap Kept = Map;
+	RecolorSmallColorClusters(Kept, Settings, 2);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("a 2-pixel region is kept when the minimum is 2"), Kept.Pixels[7].Index, 2);
+	// Off: nothing changes.
+	FFrameMap Off = Map;
+	RecolorSmallColorClusters(Off, Settings, 0);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("clusters off leaves the speck alone"), Off.Pixels[7].Index, 2);
+	return true;
+}
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxFadeOutTest, "GBPixelVfx.Temporal.FadeOut",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBPixelVfxFadeOutTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
+	auto SolidMap = []() -> FFrameMap
+	{
+		FFrameMap Map;
+		Map.Width = 8;
+		Map.Height = 8;
+		Map.Pixels.SetNum(64);
+		for (int32 i = 0; i < 64; ++i)
+		{
+			Map.Pixels[i].bSolid = true;
+			Map.Pixels[i].Ramp = 0;
+			Map.Pixels[i].Index = 1;
+			Map.Pixels[i].Value = 1.f;
+		}
+		return Map;
+	};
+	// Dissolve: removals are monotonic (frame-space pattern) and grow with t.
+	FSettings Dissolve;
+	Dissolve.bLoop = false;
+	Dissolve.FadeOutFrames = 2;
+	Dissolve.FadeOutMode = TEXT("Dissolve");
+	TArray<FFrameMap> DissolveMaps;
+	DissolveMaps.Add(SolidMap());
+	DissolveMaps.Add(SolidMap());
+	DissolveMaps.Add(SolidMap());
+	ApplyFadeOut(DissolveMaps, Dissolve);
+	const int32 First = DissolveMaps[1].SolidCount();
+	const int32 Last = DissolveMaps[2].SolidCount();
+	TestTrue(TEXT("dissolve removes pixels over the fade"), First < 64 && Last < First);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("dissolve leaves pre-fade frames alone"), DissolveMaps[0].SolidCount(), 64);
+	// Removed pixels never come back: every solid pixel of the last frame is solid in the first faded frame.
+	for (int32 i = 0; i < 64; ++i)
+	{
+		if (DissolveMaps[2].Pixels[i].bSolid)
+		{
+			TestTrue(TEXT("dissolve removals never return"), DissolveMaps[1].Pixels[i].bSolid);
+		}
+	}
+	// Erode: a solid block shrinks from the transparent border inward.
+	FSettings Erode;
+	Erode.bLoop = false;
+	Erode.FadeOutFrames = 1;
+	Erode.FadeOutMode = TEXT("Erode");
+	TArray<FFrameMap> ErodeMaps;
+	FFrameMap Block = SolidMap();
+	Block.Pixels[0].bSolid = false; // one transparent corner seeds the erosion
+	ErodeMaps.Add(Block);
+	ApplyFadeOut(ErodeMaps, Erode);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("erode removes the border first"), !ErodeMaps[0].Pixels[1].bSolid || !ErodeMaps[0].Pixels[8].bSolid);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("erode keeps the deep interior"), ErodeMaps[0].Pixels[63].bSolid);
+	// Looping clips never fade.
+	FSettings Looped;
+	Looped.bLoop = true;
+	Looped.FadeOutFrames = 2;
+	Looped.FadeOutMode = TEXT("Dissolve");
+	TArray<FFrameMap> LoopedMaps;
+	LoopedMaps.Add(SolidMap());
+	LoopedMaps.Add(SolidMap());
+	ApplyFadeOut(LoopedMaps, Looped);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("looping clips do not fade"), LoopedMaps[0].SolidCount(), 64);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("looping clips do not fade (last)"), LoopedMaps[1].SolidCount(), 64);
+	return true;
+}
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxPercentilePeakTest, "GBPixelVfx.Levels.PercentilePeak",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBPixelVfxPercentilePeakTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
+	// Ten visible pixels with luminance 0..9: the 50th percentile (nearest rank) is 4,
+	// so an implementation using the maximum (9) fails the value check below.
+	FSettings Settings = GBPixelVfxTestLocal::MakeRampSettings();
+	Settings.LevelsPercentile = 0.5f;
+	Settings.AlphaMode = TEXT("Opacity");
+	Settings.AlphaThreshold = 0.0f;
+	FWorkFrame Frame;
+	Frame.Width = 10;
+	Frame.Height = 1;
+	Frame.Color.SetNum(10);
+	Frame.Opacity.SetNum(10);
+	for (int32 i = 0; i < 10; ++i)
+	{
+		const float X = static_cast<float>(i);
+		Frame.Color[i] = FLinearColor(X, X, X);
+		Frame.Opacity[i] = 1.f;
+	}
+	float Peak = 1.f;
+	float Hot = 1.f;
+	float Cold = 1.f;
+	ComputePeaks({ Frame }, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("the percentile peak is 4, not the max"),
+		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Peak, 4.f, 0.01f));
+	return true;
+}
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxLightTrackTest, "GBPixelVfx.Light.TrackEnergyAnchor",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBPixelVfxLightTrackTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
+	// Two 2x1 frames: frame 0 has one hot pixel (v = 1) at U = 0.25, frame 1 has two
+	// hot pixels (v = 1 each) at U = 0.25 and 0.75. Energies are 1 and 2, so the
+	// normalized track is [0.5, 1]; the anchor averages the per-frame centroids.
+	FSettings Settings = GBPixelVfxTestLocal::MakeRampSettings();
+	Settings.bLightEnabled = true;
+	FFrameMap F0;
+	F0.Width = 2;
+	F0.Height = 1;
+	F0.Pixels.SetNum(2);
+	F0.Pixels[0].bSolid = true;
+	F0.Pixels[0].bHot = true;
+	F0.Pixels[0].Ramp = 0;
+	F0.Pixels[0].Index = 1;
+	F0.Pixels[0].Value = 1.f;
+	FFrameMap F1;
+	F1.Width = 2;
+	F1.Height = 1;
+	F1.Pixels.SetNum(2);
+	F1.Pixels[0].bSolid = true;
+	F1.Pixels[0].bHot = true;
+	F1.Pixels[0].Ramp = 0;
+	F1.Pixels[0].Index = 1;
+	F1.Pixels[0].Value = 1.f;
+	F1.Pixels[1].bSolid = true;
+	F1.Pixels[1].bHot = true;
+	F1.Pixels[1].Ramp = 0;
+	F1.Pixels[1].Index = 1;
+	F1.Pixels[1].Value = 1.f;
+	TArray<float> Track;
+	float AnchorU = 0.f;
+	float AnchorV = 0.f;
+	ComputeLightTrack({ F0, F1 }, Settings, Track, AnchorU, AnchorV);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("two frames produce two track values"), Track.Num(), 2);
+	if (Track.Num() == 2)
+	{
+		// A constant-lighting implementation returning 1 for both fails here.
+		TestTrue(TEXT("dimmer frame has lower energy"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Track[0], 0.5f, 0.01f));
+		TestTrue(TEXT("brightest frame normalizes to 1"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Track[1], 1.f, 0.01f));
+	}
+	// Frame centroids: 0.25 and 0.5; the anchor averages them to 0.375.
+	TestTrue(TEXT("the anchor U averages the frame centroids"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(AnchorU, 0.375f, 0.01f));
+	TestTrue(TEXT("the anchor V is the row middle"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(AnchorV, 0.5f, 0.01f));
+	return true;
+}
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBPixelVfxFractionalAlphaTest, "GBPixelVfx.Alpha.FractionalUnpremultiply",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBPixelVfxFractionalAlphaTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	using namespace GBPixelVfxConvert;
+	// Coverage 0.5 over HDR red (2, 0, 0): premultiplied RGB is (1, 0, 0), so the
+	// un-premultiplied color is (2, 0, 0) with luminance ~0.43. An implementation that
+	// multiplies by alpha again would see (0.5, 0, 0) and map a much darker band.
+	FSettings Settings = GBPixelVfxTestLocal::MakeRampSettings();
+	Settings.AlphaMode = TEXT("Opacity");
+	FWorkFrame Frame;
+	Frame.Width = 1;
+	Frame.Height = 1;
+	Frame.Color.SetNum(1);
+	Frame.Opacity.SetNum(1);
+	Frame.Color[0] = FLinearColor(1.f, 0.f, 0.f);
+	Frame.Opacity[0] = 0.5f;
+	float Peak = 1.f;
+	float Hot = 1.f;
+	float Cold = 1.f;
+	ComputePeaks({ Frame }, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("the peak sees the un-premultiplied color"),
+		FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Peak, 0.4252f, 0.01f));
+	const FFrameMap Map = MapFrame(Frame, Settings, Peak, Hot, Cold);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("fractional coverage is solid"), Map.Pixels[0].bSolid);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("fractional coverage maps to the top band"),
+		Map.Pixels[0].Value > 0.99f && Map.Pixels[0].Index == Settings.Ramps[0].Num() - 1);
+	return true;
+}

+					Verdict->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
+					VerdictActor->SetRootComponent(Verdict);
+					Verdict->RegisterComponent();

+					Verdict->CaptureSource = ESceneCaptureSource::SCS_SceneColorHDR;

+					VerdictRT->InitCustomFormat(640, 360, PF_FloatRGBA, true);
+					VerdictRT->UpdateResourceImmediate(true);
+					Verdict->TextureTarget = VerdictRT;
+					Verdict->CaptureScene();

+					{
+						TArray<FLinearColor> Raw;
+						if (Res->ReadLinearColorPixels(Raw) && Raw.Num() == 640 * 360)
+						{
+							float MaxLum = 0.f;
+							double SumLum = 0.0;
+							int32 Bright = 0;
+							for (const FLinearColor& Px : Raw)
+							{
+								const float Lum = Px.R * 0.2126f + Px.G * 0.7152f + Px.B * 0.0722f;
+								MaxLum = FMath::Max(MaxLum, Lum);
+								SumLum += Lum;
+								if (Lum > 1.f)
+								{
+									++Bright;
+								}
+							}
+							UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX showcase TEMP verdict: maxlum=%.2f meanlum=%.4f brightpx=%d of %d"),
+								MaxLum, SumLum / Raw.Num(), Bright, Raw.Num());
+						}
+						else
+						{
+							UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX showcase TEMP verdict: readback failed"));
+						}
+					}
+					VerdictActor->Destroy();
+				}
+			}
 			TakeStageScreenshot(Dir / TEXT("showcase_1.png"));
 			StageTimeMark = FPlatformTime::Seconds();

+			const FVector ProbeAt(0.f, Y0, 150.f);
+			const FVector ToCam = StageCam->GetActorLocation() - ProbeAt;
+			if (UStaticMesh* PlaneMesh = Cast<UStaticMesh>(StaticLoadObject(UStaticMesh::StaticClass(), nullptr,

+				const FVector YAxis = FVector::CrossProduct(ZAxis, XAxis);
+				const FRotator ProbeRot = FMatrix(XAxis, YAxis, ZAxis, FVector::ZeroVector).Rotator();
+				FActorSpawnParameters ProbeParams;

+				{
+					UStaticMeshComponent* ProbeMesh = NewObject<UStaticMeshComponent>(ProbeActor, NAME_None, RF_Transient);
+					ProbeMesh->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
+					ProbeActor->SetRootComponent(ProbeMesh);
+					ProbeMesh->SetStaticMesh(PlaneMesh);
+					ProbeMesh->SetRelativeScale3D(FVector(2.5f, 3.5f, 1.f));
+					ProbeMesh->RegisterComponent();
+					StageActors.Add(ProbeActor);
+					UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX showcase TEMP plane probe spawned"));
+				}
+			}
+			else
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGBPixelVfx, Display, TEXT("PVFX showcase TEMP plane mesh LOAD FAILED"));
+			}

+    # TEMP DIAG: dump the Plane mesh health. REMOVE AFTER.
+    for path in ["/Engine/BasicShapes/Plane.Plane", "/Engine/BasicShapes/Cube.Cube"]:
+        asset = unreal.load_asset(path)
+        if asset is None:
+            log("DIAG %s: LOAD FAILED" % path)
+            continue
+        try:
+            lod0 = asset.get_editor_property("static_mesh_lod_group")
+        except Exception:
+            lod0 = "?"
+        num_verts = 0
+        num_tris = 0
+        num_uvs = 0
+        try:
+            rd = asset.get_editor_property("render_data")
+            if rd is not None:
+                lod = rd.get_editor_property("lod_resources")[0]
+                num_verts = lod.get_editor_property("vertex_buffers").static_mesh_vertex_buffer.get_num_vertices()
+                num_tris = lod.get_num_triangles()
+                num_uvs = lod.get_editor_property("vertex_buffers").static_mesh_vertex_buffer.get_num_tex_coords()
+        except Exception as e:
+            log("DIAG %s: render data read failed: %s" % (path, e))
+        log("DIAG %s: verts=%s tris=%s uvs=%s lodgroup=%s" % (path, num_verts, num_tris, num_uvs, lod0))
     log("OK")
